Credits expire 6 months from account creation on the new sign-up experience (FAQ); 12 months per the July 2025 Free Tier Terms for earlier sign-ups.
  - *Gotchas:* Suspended after expiry — move data/projects before the 90-day window or lose them. Free plan is 'evaluation only' per terms (sensitive data processing disallowed). Credits can't be transferred to other accounts. AWS may reclaim resources after 3 months of inactivity with 30 days' notice. Unused monthly free amounts don't roll over. Activating paid-only services upgrades you to Paid plan (bills on pay-as-you-go beyond credits). EC2/RDS/S3/API Gateway on new accounts are no longer fixed monthly free allowances — they consume the $200 credit pool; new free-plan-eligible EC2 types: t3.micro, t3.small, t4g.micro, t4g.small, c7i-flex.large, m7i-flex.large (t2.micro is legacy only).
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/free/free-tier-faqs/
- **AWS Free Tier — 30+ Always Free services (all customers, indefinite)** (high) — All AWS customers (new and existing, Free and Paid plans) get over 30 'Always Free' services with monthly usage caps; usage is aggregated across all regions; excess usage automatically applies Free Tier credits (Free plan) or bills at standard on-demand rates (Paid plan). Representative always-free caps (per service docs and community verification, not all published on the /free/ page itself): Lambda 1M requests + 400,000 GB-seconds; DynamoDB 25 GB + 25 provisioned RCU / 5 WCU; SQS 1M requests; SNS 1M publishes; CloudWatch 10 custom metrics + 10 alarms + 1M API requests; CloudFront 1 TB data transfer out.
  - *Eligibility:* Any AWS customer — no .edu, no student status, no credit card required for the account itself. Available in regions where AWS operates (Always Free offers are region-broader than the Free Plan credits, which exclude China and GovCloud).
  - *How to claim:* Automatic once you have an AWS account; no application.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry while you hold an AWS account; monthly limits reset each month with no rollover. AWS may terminate any offer at any time.
  - *Gotchas:* Data transfer out (egress) beyond CloudFront's 1 TB and inter-region traffic are billable; paid add-on features auto-bill on Paid plans; individual service caps vary — check each service's pricing page before heavy use. The per-service figures above are medium-confidence (sourced from the EC2 docs and builder-community summaries, since the /free/ page lists service tiles without caps).
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/free/
- **AWS Free Tier — short-term trials on selected Paid-plan services** (medium) — Selected Paid-plan services can be 'tried' with limited free trials; the trial begins when you activate the service, and any eligible Free Tier credits automatically apply to usage beyond trial limits. Some offers are limited in duration or free-usage amount; on a Free plan, activating a paid-only service upgrades the account to Paid plan.
  - *Eligibility:* New AWS customers (per-service trials listed on aws.amazon.com/free/); no .edu required; same region caveats (Free Plan credits unavailable in China/GovCloud).
  - *How to claim:* Activate the trial service from the console; track the trial end date yourself — AWS does not always notify when a trial ends.
  - *Expiry:* Per-service trial windows; AWS may end any offer at any time.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial vs Always Free confusion: trial periods end and pay-as-you-go billing starts without a notification in some cases — delete trial resources before expiry. Egress/data-transfer and public IPv4 charges ($0.005/hr) commonly surprise trial users.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/free/
- **AWS Educate — free self-paced curriculum, hands-on labs, badges, job board** (high) — Free, self-paced online cloud training 'designed for beginners': hundreds of hours of content incl. Getting Started with Storage (S3), Compute (EC2), Networking (VPC), Databases (RDS), and Cloud Operations + cost-estimation labs, a Cloud 101 pathway, and a Cloud Literacy interactive badge. Includes free hands-on labs in a sandboxed AWS Console environment (time-boxed, unlimited retries), shareable Credly digital badges, an invitation to the AWS Emerging Talent Community, and an AWS Educate Job Board (18+) with in-demand jobs and internships. No dollar-denominated AWS cloud credits — it is a learning program, not a credit grant.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to any individual regardless of education, experience, or career journey — NO .edu email or university affiliation required. Register with just an email address; no credit card needed. Minimum age: 'as young as 13' per the current page; the Educate FAQ says 14+ generally, with EU 16+; Algeria, Lebanon, Portugal 18+; not offered in China or Switzerland.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Register at the AWS Educate sign-up page (promo code 'EducateLP' referenced on the page) or sign in to AWS Educate. 2) Choose a learning path (Cloud 101 recommended for new-to-cloud). 3) Complete labs/courses. 4) Claim digital badges on Credly. 5) Job board access at 18+.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; content and lab time limits update over time; lab attempts are time-boxed but retries are unlimited.
  - *Gotchas:* Labs run on shared sandboxes, not your own AWS account (does not stack with your Free Tier account). No monetary credits. Employer direct-messaging on the job board is US-only per the FAQ. Age minimum varies by country (higher in EU and some regions than the '13+' marketing). No certification exam included — AWS certifications remain paid (see AWS Academy vouchers).
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/education/awseducate/
- **AWS Skillbuilder — 500-600+ free self-paced digital training courses** (high) — AWS's Training FAQ: 'Self-paced digital training on AWS Skill Builder is free.' Free digital training includes 500+ on-demand courses in 17 languages (the /training/ page markets '600+ free digital courses'); free digital badges (knowledge, Cloud Quest) earned at >=80% on the associated assessment, claimable on Credly. Paid Individual/Team subscriptions add more content and labs; free webinars/Twitch training events also exist, while classroom training and certification exams cost money.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone — no .edu or student status; sign in with a free AWS Builder ID (or APN credentials / company Team subscription if an org member). No credit card.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Open AWS Skill Builder and sign in or create a free AWS Builder ID. 2) Enroll in any free digital course. 3) Score >=80% on the assessment to earn a badge. 4) Claim the badge via Credly.
  - *Expiry:* No stated expiry for free access; subscription content changes with plans.
  - *Gotchas:* Only part of Skillbuilder is free — advanced lab-heavy content is behind paid Individual/Team subscriptions; certification exams are paid (vouchers only via AWS Academy pathways); don't confuse free digital badges with paid AWS certification badges.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/training/faqs/
- **AWS Activate — Founders package: $1,000 credits (select participants up to $5,000)** (high) — For self-funded, early-stage startups new to AWS Activate Credits: $1,000 USD in AWS credits (per AWS's gaming-startups page: 'Start with $1,000 USD in Activate Credits. Select participants may qualify for additional credits up to $5,000'). Credits redeem on 200+ eligible AWS services incl. third-party foundation models on Amazon Bedrock. Also includes technical support, business mentorship, co-build/co-market/go-to-market resources, free pre-built cloud templates, AWS Startup Advisor, AWS Support Plans eligible for credits, and access to 80+ third-party offers. New customers also get the AWS Free Tier when creating a new account.
  - *Eligibility:* Startup program — NOT an education/.edu program (student-founded startups can apply as startups). Criteria: self-funded (or pre-Series B per AWS's June 2026 guide), founded in the past 10 years, a fully functioning company website or public company profile, new to Activate Credits (or requesting more than previously received), and a business email matching the company domain. No student-specific grant exists.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a personal AWS Builder ID with a professional email. 2) Complete the AWS Activate profile. 3) Choose the Founders package. 4) Submit company/product/funding details (accurate info required; inaccuracies can be denied). 5) Link an AWS account and submit; decision in 7-10 business days by email, trackable in the console.
  - *Expiry:* Credits are subject to the AWS Promotional Credit Terms & Conditions (fixed validity from grant, not re-stated on these pages); not renewable at the same value without a larger request.
  - *Gotchas:* Without a functioning startup/website you won't qualify. AWS has final say; website professionalism is weighted heavily. Credits don't cover Marketplace third-party services, Route 53 domain registration/transfer, most support tiers, or Professional Services (except Bedrock third-party model charges). AWS offers generally not available in China regions.
  - *Source:* https://startups.aws.com/lp/aws-activate-credits
- **AWS Activate — Portfolio package: up to $200,000 credits (provider-backed)** (medium) — For startups affiliated with an AWS Activate Provider (accelerator, VC, incubator, or partner org — e.g., Y Combinator, a16z, nVIDIA, Carta): up to $200,000 in AWS Activate Credits per the main /startups/ page and AWS's June 4, 2026 step-by-step guide ('The Activate Portfolio tier offers up to $200,000 in AWS Activate Credits for provider-backed startups'); AI startups ready to scale can get additional credits beyond $200,000 via their Account Manager. Also: 80+ exclusive third-party offers/memberships valued up to $800,000, dedicated account managers, and solutions architects. Note: the /free/offers/ comparison page still lists the range as '$1,000 to $100,000' with eligibility 'funded up to and including Series A' — the $200k / pre-Series-B figures come from AWS's newer 2026 pages, indicating the cap and funding stage were raised in 2025-2026.
  - *Eligibility:* Must hold an Organizational ID from an AWS Activate Provider; pre-Series B (most recent funding round within the last 12 months, if applicable); founded in the past 10 years; a fully functioning company website; business email; must be new to Activate Credits or requesting more than previously received. Not open to direct application — provider affiliation is mandatory. No .edu/student path.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Enroll in a program with an AWS Activate Provider and get their Org ID/invitation. 2) Create an AWS Builder ID + complete the AWS Activate profile. 3) Choose the Portfolio package with your Org ID. 4) Submit details; link an AWS account; decision in 7-10 business days.
  - *Expiry:* Per the AWS Promotional Credit Terms & Conditions (fixed validity from grant).
  - *Gotchas:* Provider gatekeeping (AWS has final say and gets pickier above $5,000). Official AWS pages conflict today on the cap: $100k on /free/offers/ vs $200k on /startups/ and the June 2026 guide — treat 'up to $200,000' as the current advertised max and verify at application. Legacy sub-tiers ($5k/$10k/$25k/$100k; YC's up to $250k) are no longer publicly listed.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/
- **AWS Academy — free participation for accredited higher-education institutions (+ exam vouchers)** (high) — AWS Academy 'provides higher education institutions with a free, ready-to-teach cloud computing curriculum' (aligned to AWS certifications) plus Learner Labs. FAQ: 'There is no cost to join.' Educators may request a 100% discount voucher on AWS certification exam cost; member institutions may request a 50% discount exam voucher that students redeem at 100% of the voucher's value; students in badge-eligible courses earn Emerging Talent Community points redeemable for rewards such as discounted AWS certification exam vouchers. This is AWS's closest university-scoped free offer.
  - *Eligibility:* Institutional, not individual: accredited higher-education institution offering degrees, diplomas, certificates, or continuing-ed programs, with an authorized Program Administrator or Central Point of Contact (CPOC). No .edu-email-only rule, but institutional membership is required; individual students/educators can't self-enroll.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Institution applies to join AWS Academy (or is added to an existing membership). 2) CPOC nominates educators; educators complete the mandatory Getting Started course. 3) Educators teach courses; students use Learner Labs. 4) Educators/students claim exam discount vouchers per program rules.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry while the institution remains a member; voucher/reward programs are 'subject to change' per the FAQ.
  - *Gotchas:* Vouchers discount exam fees only — they are not AWS usage credits. Institutional onboarding can be slow; the free value to students is mainly the curriculum, labs, and exam discounts — hands-on AWS usage still comes from the regular Free Tier (which needs no Academy affiliation).
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/training/awsacademy/faq/
- **2025–26 change:** SUNSET / MATERIAL CHANGES 2024-2026: (1) The classic AWS 12-month free tier (EC2 750 hrs/mo, S3 5 GB, 1M input + 1M output free Bedrock model-invocation tokens per month for 12 months across ~75 models, RDS 750 hrs, etc.) has been SUPERSEDED by the new Free plan / Paid plan model: $100 signup credits + up to $100 earned, 6-month window, 30+ 'always free' services, and notably no payment method required for most new customers. The Bedrock-specific free-token docs page (docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/free-trial.html) now renders empty/removed, and the new Free Tier FAQ contains zero mention of Bedrock free tokens, 750 EC2 hours, or 5 GB S3 - legacy per-service allowances no longer exist. So the 'free Bedrock tokens for students' front is effectively dead; Bedrock access now comes via (a) Free plan $100-200 credits, (b) AWS Activate credits, or (c) Kiro credits for agentic coding. (2) June 2024: AWS doubled the portfolio/accelerate credit ceiling from $100,000 to $200,000 (announced July 1, 2024, for startups that raised Series A in the past 12 months; seed stage stayed at $100,000 at the time; credits' expiry moved from 1 year to 3 years - per CNBC 2024-06-28). The current credits page (2026) shows one Portfolio tier at up to $200,000. (3) 2026: the credits page was redesigned (awslabs/startups PR #75, June 2026) renaming tiers to Activate Founders ($1,000 start, up to $5,000 - NOT the ~$100 sometimes misreported; the $100 figure matches the new Free plan signup credit, not Activate), Activate Portfolio (up to $200,000), and adding the invite-only 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' tier ($200,000+) plus the new Kiro Startup Credits program. (4) 'Known so far' figures from the task (~$100K Portfolio, ~$100 Founders) are outdated: Portfolio is now up to $200K and Founders is $1,000-to-start/up-to-$5,000. No standalone student Bedrock/LLM free offer from AWS exists today - the closest student fronts are the no-card Kiro Student Offer (1,000 credits/month x 12 months) and AWS Academy (training only, no credits). Verification caveat: today's live fetches of aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/ and free sub-pages intermittently returned 403 via proxy, but credits-page content was captured today via the official page mirror in the awslabs/startups repo and search snippets of the live page; free-plan figures came directly from aws.amazon.com/free/ and /free/free-tier-faqs/ read today.</sunset_notes>
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025-2026 changes (all verified on official AWS pages fetched today, 2026-10-22; WebSearch budget was exhausted so only official-page fetches were used): (1) The legacy usage-based 12-month AWS Free Tier (750 RDS hours, 5 GB S3, etc.) was replaced by a credit-based model for accounts created on/after July 15, 2025 — $100 at sign-up + up to $100 earned, Free plan (6-month auto-closing account) vs Paid plan (12-month credit validity); a refreshed sign-up experience launched October 2026, and pre-October accounts still see the old credits console. The RDS pricing page still documents the legacy 750-hour/20 GB free database for pre-July-15-2025 accounts only. (2) Per-service 'Always Free' and legacy free-trial figures (e.g., Athena GB-per-month, Redshift RA3/RA2 1-node 12-month developer tier and 30-day standard tier, Glue 1M source-records ETL free allowance, Bedrock character-based free tiers) no longer appear on today's service pricing pages — the credit model absorbed them, so do not quote legacy numbers for new accounts. (3) Activate program structure is now three tiers: Founders ($1,000, up to $5,000), Portfolio (up to $200,000 via provider Org ID), and an invite-only 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' tier adding $200,000+ for AI startups scaling past the Portfolio ceiling. Credits now explicitly cover Amazon Bedrock third-party models (OpenAI, Mistral, Anthropic, Meta). Note a discrepancy: /free/offers/ still lists Activate as '$1,000 to $100,000' — likely stale vs the $200,000 ceiling on the credits page/FAQ. (4) New Kiro startup credits program (up to a year of Kiro Pro+, 2/10/30-user tiers, 'limited time only', excludes France/Germany/Italy/Spain/Poland/UAE and ~13 other countries). (5) AWS Academy / Cloud Career Academies: no working page found on today's main AWS site (URLs 404; cloudcareeracademies.org DNS-failed) — status unconfirmed; AWS Educate (free, 13+, no credits/vouchers) is explicitly still active with a 2026 copyright. Also of note: AWS Educate bundles no cloud credits and no free exam vouchers on any page I could read today.
- **2025–26 change:** (1) AWS Free Tier revamp: the classic structure - 12 months of Free Trial on ~75 services plus a separate 'Always Free' tier - is no longer on the official page; it has been replaced (2025-2026) by the Free plan / Paid plan model with up to $200 in credits, 90+ services for up to 6 months, and '30+ services always free within monthly usage limits'. The exact launch date of the revamp could not be confirmed (web-search budget exhausted mid-session); the page read today (2026-10-22) shows only the new structure. (2) AWS Activate: the legacy 'standard program up to $1,000 in credits' copy no longer appears on the live portal; the join page now says 'Apply for up to $200,000 in Activate Credits' while the application widget caps at 'up to $100,000' - consistent with a headline increase beyond the old $100K Accelerate ceiling, unconfirmed via a dated press release. (3) AWS Impact Accelerator, the US-only predecessor (part of AWS's 2022 $30M commitment to Black, Women, Latino and LGBTQIA+ founders), was superseded by the global AWS Impact Bootcamps announced Jul 30, 2025; the 2022-2023 US cohorts are closed. (4) All AWS Impact Bootcamps application windows through the 2026 Tokyo and Berlin cohorts are closed (deadlines Feb 27 and Mar 13, 2026), so as of 2026-10-22 the $5,000 credits, $50K grant and $15K credits are only obtainable in future, not-yet-announced cohorts. (5) Research caveat: https://startups.aws.com/lp/aws-impact-bootcamps currently returns HTTP 500 at the server level (its Next.js content payload is intact and was extracted from the raw HTML); the shared WebSearch budget for this session was exhausted, so 'was this changed in 2025-2026' checks rely on today's live pages rather than dated announcements.
- **2025–26 change:** Key 2025-2026 changes: (1) 2025-07-15 cutover - AWS replaced the legacy 12-month service-level Free Tier with a credit-based model: new accounts get a 6-month 'Free plan' ($100 signup credits + up to $100 service-exploration credits = up to $200) or a Paid plan; accounts created before 2025-07-15 are grandfathered on the old 12-month per-service free tier (e.g. RDS: 750 hours/month single-AZ, 20 GB gp2 storage, 20 GB backup). (2) Free-plan accounts auto-close at 6 months or when credits run out; data retained 90 days, then permanently erased. (3) Startup program restructuring: the /startups/credits/ page now lists 'Activate Founders' (up to $5,000; starts at $1,000) and 'Activate Portfolio' (up to $200,000, was the $150,000 Founders' Program era ceiling; the previous 'Accelerate' up to $100,000 tier name no longer appears) plus two newer tracks - 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' ($200,000+, invite-only post-Portfolio) and 'Kiro Startup Credits'. The exact 2025-2026 rename/retirement dates could not be confirmed: WebSearch budget for this session was exhausted, so only the current official pages could be verified. (4) No .edu-gated student dollar-credit license was found: the education front is AWS Educate (free training/badges, no credits) and institutional 'special pricing' programs; AWS Academy pages returned 404 on fetched sub-paths, so its current status/offerings are unverified rather than confirmed active or sunsetting. (5) Free-plan credits are unavailable in China regions or AWS GovCloud (US); the Free plan also excludes all promotional credits (Activate), so startup credits effectively require a Paid-plan account.
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025-2026 changes: (1) July 15, 2025 - the classic 12-month per-service Free Tier was replaced for NEW accounts with a credit-based model: $100 sign-up credit + up to $100 earned via 5 console activities, a 6-month Free plan window (account closes at 6 months or when credits run out), 90+ services, and 30+ always-free services; accounts created before July 15, 2025 retain the legacy 12-month tier. Free-tier-eligible EC2 instances changed too (t2.micro dropped; t3.small, t4g.small, c7i-flex.large, m7i-flex.large added). (2) Short-term free trials are now paid-plan-only; the Free plan only gets 'Always Free' offers. (3) Feb 21, 2023 - AWS Educate stopped its old monetary grants ($35/$100 student grants, $75/$200 educator grants); educator tooling moved to AWS Academy. (4) Feb 16, 2026 - Free plan (Free Tier) accounts became ineligible for promotional credits, which affects AWS Cloud Credit for Research and similar programs (a Paid plan is required to hold awards). (5) Aug 20, 2026 - AWS launched Student Rewards on AWS Builder Center as part of a $500M+ student commitment; the old 'AWS Activate for Students' style offer no longer appears on aws.amazon.com - student benefits are now Builder Center Student Rewards + AWS Educate + AWS Academy. (6) October 2026 - new AWS sign-up experience launched; credit-expiry display moved to the Settings Portal (accounts that signed up before Oct 2026 still use the Credits page in the Billing console). No free offer listed above was fully sunset as of 2026-10-22.
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025-2026 changes: (1) July 15, 2025 — AWS replaced the long-standing 12-month Free Tier (750 hrs/mo t2.micro/t3.micro + 30 GB EBS + 100 GB egress, 30+ always-free services) with a credit-based model for NEW accounts: $100 sign-up credits + up to $100 earned = up to $200, with a 6-month no-cost Free plan that auto-closes. Legacy 12-month benefits remain ONLY for accounts created before July 15, 2025. Also, since the revamp, a credit card/payment method is no longer required to sign up for most new customers (previously mandatory). A new sign-up experience (Settings Portal credit tracking) launched October 2026. (2) June 2026 — AWS Activate Portfolio credit ceiling doubled from $100,000 to $200,000; the Founders tier was standardized at $1,000 with an 'up to $5,000' extension for select participants; a new invite-only 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' tier (>$200K) was introduced (confirmed by the redesigned aws.amazon.com/startups/credits page © 2026 and the awslabs/startups repo content update June 16, 2026). Note: CNBC's June 28, 2024 report that Series A startups got $200K from July 1, 2024 was NOT reflected in 2025 official materials (which still said 'up to $100,000'); the cap only officially moved to $200K in June 2026. (3) April 2025 — AWS launched the $30M Impact Accelerator (new program, not a sunset). (4) No VPS/bare-metal/dedicated specific free offer was sunset, because none existed as a standalone front: bare-metal and dedicated hosts have never had free tiers and remain credit-eligible under Activate/Free Tier credits only. (5) GAIA 2025 cohort status and Impact Accelerator 2026 track openings could not be re-verified this session (shared web-search budget exhausted); confidence ratings above reflect that.
- **2025–26 change:** Sunset/changed in 2025-2026: (1) 15 Jul 2025 (announced AWS Summit NY, 15-16 Jul 2025): the 12-month Free Tier was retired for NEW accounts and replaced by the credit-based Free Plan ($100 signup credit + up to $100 explore credits, 6-month auto-closing plan, 90-day post-expiry data retention); pre-cutoff accounts keep their legacy 12-month offers until each one's own expiry. (2) EC2 free-VPS offer sunset: t2.micro (and hours-based t3.micro 750 h/mo) free tiers no longer apply to new accounts; new free-tier-eligible EC2 types are t3.micro, t3.small, t4g.micro, t4g.small, c7i-flex.large, m7i-flex.large, consumed via credits. (3) AWS Activate was folded into AWS for Startups: /aws.amazon.com/activate/ now serves the startups program (startups.aws.com); headline credit figure is 'up to $200,000' with published tiers $1,000-$100,000, 80+ partner offers valued up to $800,000, plus undisclosed extra AI-startup credits and Kiro credits — replacing older multi-year $1M total caps and old student/faculty Activate tiers (up to $100k/$150k) which no longer appear on official pages. (4) The Free Plan explicitly cannot combine with any other promotional/incentive offer, and joining AWS Organizations/Control Tower auto-upgrades to Paid and voids remaining explore credits. (5) AWS signals a new sign-up experience in October 2026 (FAQ note); Free Tier Terms last updated 9 Jul 2025. Note: session web-search budget was exhausted, so 2025-2026 change verification relied on WebFetch of official AWS pages (free/, free/offers/, free/free-tier-faqs/, free/terms/, startups/, education/, EC2 docs, Lambda pricing, RDS free page, what's-new Jul 2025 entry, AWS blog 15 Jul 2025) rather than broader search.
- **2025–26 change:** Material 2025-2026 changes (all verified against AWS pages today, 2026-10-22): (1) Jul 15, 2025 — the legacy "12 months free" tier was replaced for NEW accounts by a credit-based Free Tier: $100 sign-up credit + up to $100 earned, 6-month Free plan; every legacy 12-month offer on pre-Jul-15-2025 accounts (including legacy CloudWatch 12-month items) has now fully lapsed. (2) Oct 2026 — AWS launched a new sign-up experience: social sign-in (Google/GitHub/Apple/Amazon), no credit card needed for most customers, and credit tracking moved from the Billing console to a Settings Portal for accounts signed up after the rollout; the Free Tier FAQ now states base credits expire 6 months (vs. 12 months in the Jul 9, 2025 Terms) — a live contradiction AWS hasn't harmonized, so treat 6 months as the safe planning horizon. (3) Dec 3, 2025 — Kiro Startup Credits v1 launched (up to 100 users, pre-seed to Series B, apply by Dec 31, 2025) and closed; relaunched Apr 7, 2026 with 2/10/30-user tiers, early-stage to Series A (VC-backed per the current promotion), apply by Dec 31, 2026; startups with active AWS Activate credits are excluded from the Kiro promo. (4) Jun 2024 (context) — AWS doubled Portfolio credits $100K to $200K and extended expiry 1 year to 3 years; still the structure in effect. (5) Feb 2023 (context) — AWS Educate stopped issuing universal/institutional credit grants; it is now free learning only, and student credits now flow through Builder Center Student Rewards (up to $579 incl. $30 credits + $100 exam voucher, launched Aug 2026). (6) CloudFront's classic "100 GB/month always-free" line is now presented as a standing $0 "Free" plan tier with 100 GB + 1M requests + 5 GB S3 storage credits and no overage charges — not sunset, but restructured. No CloudWatch/observability offer was sunset; the known limits (10 metrics, 1M API requests, 5 GB logs, 100 canaries, 3 dashboards, 10 alarms, 100K X-Ray traces) all still apply as ALWAYS-FREE allowances. There is no CloudWatch-specific startup or student grant beyond the general AWS Free Tier credits and Activate credits, which can offset CloudWatch usage on a Paid plan.
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025-2026 changes verified on official pages today (2026-10-22): (1) The legacy 12-month Free Tier is gone — the old 'free for 12 months' language no longer appears on aws.amazon.com/free; it has been replaced by the credit-based Free Plan ($100 signup + up to $100 explore = up to $200 over 6 months), after which the account auto-closes (data kept 90 days, then erased). (2) The classic 'Always Free' list was narrowed in messaging to '30+ services' with monthly limits (legacy tier had 60+ services); per-service quota numbers are no longer published on the hub. (3) SageMaker notebook free allowance shrunk to 250 hours of sc.t3.medium for the first 2 months (vs legacy 750 hours/month ml.t3.medium for 12 months). (4) AWS Activate tiers renamed/revalued: 'Activate Community' no longer appears on the current credits page — it is now 'Activate Founders' (up to $5,000, start $1,000) alongside 'Activate Portfolio' (now up to $200,000, previously up to $100,000) plus a new invite-only 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' ($200,000+); a new Kiro Startup Credits track (up to 1 year of Kiro Pro+, 1/10/30 users) was added. The stale /free/offers page still shows old $1,000-$100,000 figures and 7-10 day processing, inconsistent with the current credits page ($5,000/$200,000, 5-10 days) — treat the credits page as authoritative. (5) WebSearch was unavailable during this run, so no third-party press confirmation of announcement dates was possible; all claims above are from the official AWS pages fetched today.
- **2025–26 change:** 1) Amazon Pinpoint sunset (HARD DATE): AWS ends support for Amazon Pinpoint on October 30, 2026 — console access and all Pinpoint resources (endpoints, segments, campaigns, journeys, analytics) terminate that day; only the messaging-channel APIs (SMS, voice, mobile push, OTP, phone number validate) continue under the rebranded "AWS End User Messaging" service. Anyone relying on Pinpoint journeys/campaigns must migrate before 2026-10-30. 2) Legacy per-service Free Tier replaced: the old model — e.g., "SES: 3,000 emails/month free for the first 12 months, then $0.10/1K" and "SNS: 100 SMS/month free, US regions" — is no longer published; as of the October 2026 sign-up experience, new accounts instead get up to $200 in Free Tier credits over 6 months (Free plan auto-closes at 6 months or when credits run out). The "then $0.10/1K" figure survives, but as the à-la-carte SES price, while NEW SES accounts default to the more expensive Essentials plan ($0.16/1K) starting July 21, 2026. 3) The formerly permanent "3,000 emails/month free (US regions, no purchase of sending capacity)" SES allowance is not listed on today's official pricing page — treat it as superseded by the credit model for new accounts; legacy account behavior not verifiable (docs pages didn't render). 4) The legacy 100-free-SMS/month US-regions tier also no longer appears on End User Messaging pricing; SMS is now fully pay-per-message. 5) AWS Activate credit ceiling was raised to $200,000 (some pages still show the old $100,000 figure — a 2025-2026 program change, not a sunset).
- **2025–26 change:** 2025-2026 changes (verified against official AWS pages on 2026-10-22): (1) Classic 12-month Free Tier sunset for NEW accounts on July 15, 2025 — replaced by the credit-based Free Plan ($100 + up to $100 earned = up to $200 over 6 months, auto-enrolled, no charges); accounts created before July 15, 2025 remain on legacy 12-month terms (750 hrs/mo t2/t3.micro etc.); legacy terms still published. (2) October 2026: AWS launched a new sign-up experience; the current FAQ now states Free Tier credits expire 6 months from account creation (vs 12 months under the July 2025 Free Tier Terms) and moved credit tracking to the Settings Portal — AWS's own FAQ is internally inconsistent between sections (6 vs 12 months), so treat credit expiry on the new experience as 6 months and verify per-account in the Settings Portal. (3) AWS Activate restructured in 2025-2026: old tiers (Starter $5k / Portfolio up to $100k / YC up to $250k / $5k-$100k sub-tiers) are no longer publicly listed; current structure is Founders ($1,000, up to $5,000 for select participants) + Portfolio (up to $200,000, cap raised from $100,000), with eligibility shifted from 'funded up to Series A' to 'pre-Series B, most recent funding round within the last 12 months', and the application flow now goes through the AWS Builder ID. AWS pages are inconsistent today: /free/offers/ still advertises '$1,000 to $100,000' while /startups/ and the June 4, 2026 AWS blog advertise 'up to $200,000'. (4) No education-only (.edu/student-email) free credits exist at AWS: no free offer requires a .edu or student email — AWS Educate is open to anyone 13/14+ with just an email, the Free Tier is open to any new customer, and AWS Marketplace 'education pricing' is a paid discount, not free. The only university-scoped free offer is AWS Academy (free institution participation + exam vouchers). (5) Free Plan/credits unavailable in China (ZHY, BJS) and GovCloud (US) regions, and not usable via AWS Organizations (auto-upgrades to Paid plan). (6) AWS Educate availability excludes China and Switzerland and raises minimum age in the EU (16) and Algeria/Lebanon/Portugal (18).
- **Critic correction** (AWS Activate (Amazon Bedrock)): Figures are out of date. The Activate Founders self-serve package is $1,000 (select participants can reach $5,000), not about $100. The Portfolio package, for startups tied to an Activate Provider with an Org ID, is now up to $200,000; it was $100,000 before June 2026. Credits can be used on Bedrock third-party models. AI startups ready to scale can talk to an account manager about more.
- **Critic correction** (AWS Startups): Range is outdated. Current tiers are Activate Founders at $1,000 (up to $5,000 for select startups) and Activate Portfolio at up to $200,000 (raised from $100,000 in June 2026). The top of the range should read $200,000 for Portfolio, not $100,000+.
- **Critic correction** (Up to ~$100K in AWS credits (Portfolio/Partner tier; ~$100 Founders self-serve)): The current official maximum is up to $200,000 for the Portfolio tier (requires a provider Org ID; pre-Series B). The Founders (self-funded) tier starts at $1,000, with up to $5,000 for select participants, not about $100.

### DigitalOcean

- **DigitalOcean Startups program (formerly Hatch) — up to $100,000 in compute credits over 12 months + 15 months free Standard-tier support** (high) — DigitalOcean Startups (rebranded from 'Hatch', program running since 2016). Official program page (read 2026-10-22): '12 months to scale' — credits for 12 months, amount varies by partner and is stated in the onboarding email, with a $10,000 monthly limit and charges for any monthly use above it. January 22, 2025 press release (investors.digitalocean.com, official) states 'up to $100,000 in Compute Credits' plus 'Complimentary Premium Support' and marketplace access to 600,000+ customers; BusinessWire's release also stated 'up to 3 Months of Free GPU Usage' for qualified startups. Current page lists 'Free access to Standard-tier paid support for 15 months.' Official ToS (last updated May 11, 2026) confirms a 12-month program term and 1-year credit window. Enrollment itself is free ('no fee to participate'); non-transferable credits.
  - *Eligibility:* Must have raised $10M or less total funding. Requires a company website with a matching company-domain email (no Gmail etc.) and a new DigitalOcean team account on that email. A valid credit card IS required on the account (for billing/overages). Startups that have previously used DigitalOcean credits are excluded. Service-based businesses (consulting, web/app dev shops, marketing agencies) are ineligible. AI-native startups are prioritized but not required. No .edu requirement. No published country restriction; apply through a partner (VC/accelerator/incubator, e.g. Techstars) or directly via the application form ('Other').
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a NEW DigitalOcean team account with a company-domain email (free-mail not accepted) and a valid credit card; 2) Apply via digitalocean.com/startups / do.co/AIStartups — through an approved partner (accelerator/VC such as Techstars) or directly by selecting 'Other'; 3) On acceptance the credit amount is set per partner and stated in the onboarding email; 4) Spend within the 12-month window, max $10,000 usable per month. 15 months of Standard-tier support and the Welcome Kit partner perks (Stripe Atlas 20% off, QuickNode up to $5k, Airtable $2,000, 100ms $2,500, Miro $1,000, Notion $1,000, Cloud66 $500, Bolster $1,000, Mercury $500 cash on $50k deposit, HubSpot up to 90% off year one, ChargeBee free until $1M revenue, Shortcut 2 months free, Ovvy 90 days free) are included on acceptance.
  - *Expiry:* Credits expire 12 months after DigitalOcean accepts you into the program (one year from acceptance per ToS, §1.2); unused credits are forfeited with no extension or rollover. Free Standard-tier support lasts 15 months. ToS auto-terminates the arrangement on acquisition, IPO, insolvency, or ceasing normal operations; liability capped at $100; no resell/sublicense; crypto mining prohibited.
  - *Gotchas:* Credit card required at signup — not 'free' in the no-CC sense. Core credits as of May 13, 2026 explicitly EXCLUDE: GPU Droplets, NVIDIA H100 GPU Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, Dedicated Inference, Serverless Inference/third-party AI models, Paperspace Machines, Cloudways, and third-party pass-through charges — GPU access is a separate invite-only benefit, and the January 2025 'up to 3 months free GPU' is no longer published. Amount is unpublished and partner-set; the $100,000 ceiling comes only from the Jan 2025 press release. Usage above $10,000 in a month is billed to your card. Powered-off Droplets still bill. Egress beyond bundled allowances is charged separately.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/startups
- **GPU credit packages ('Early-Stage Boost') — separate, invite-only GPU credits for accepted startups** (high) — Official page states 'Selected startups will be invited to apply for GPU credit packages.' under 'Early-Stage Boost'; GPU credits are 'a separate benefit' from the core credits, and core credits do not cover GPU Droplet usage — your payment method is charged for it. The H100 pool / GPU benefits were a headline of the Jan 22, 2025 announcement ('generous credits for our GPU-powered Droplets and other AI/ML resources', 'priority access to GPU power').
  - *Eligibility:* Accepted DigitalOcean Startups participants (i.e., same eligibility as the core program: <= $10M raised, new DO customer, company-domain email, credit card on account, product not service business), then invitation from the DigitalOcean team. No .edu requirement.
  - *How to claim:* Invitation-only: acceptance into the program does not guarantee GPU credits. The DO team reviews application and infrastructure needs, invites selected startups to submit a separate GPU credit application. In 2025, per the press-release package, qualified startups got 'up to 3 Months of Free GPU Usage' (terms apply), with H100 GPUs available for AI/ML workloads; no amount/duration is currently published on the official page. To run H100s without GPU credits you pay list price (~$1.90/GPU-hour, third-party figure — unverified on official pages today).
  - *Expiry:* GPU credit package terms (amount/duration) not published; determined per invitation. Core program credit window is 12 months from acceptance.
  - *Gotchas:* Since the May 13, 2026 exclusion update, GPU Droplets, H100 GPU Kubernetes and Bare Metal GPUs are off the core credit table — teams relying on GPUs must get the separate GPU package or pay list. Acceptance into the main program does NOT guarantee the GPU package. Exclusions also cover third-party model hosting on inference products.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/startups
- **New-account trial credit: $5 for 90 days (current trial; replaced the older $200/60-day offer)** (high) — Official pricing page FAQ (read 2026-10-22): 'if you received a $5 credit for 90 days, then that $5 credit is automatically applied to your account.' Credit card required at signup (a ~$1 temporary pre-authorization is taken and reversed). No perpetual free compute tier exists: Droplets start at $4/month, managed databases from $15/month, Spaces from $5/month. Billing is per-second with a 60-second/$0.01 minimum.
  - *Eligibility:* Any new DigitalOcean account (all regions where DigitalOcean is available). A payment method (credit card) IS required before creating resources — no CC-free path. No .edu, no age/funding limits. Applies once, to the first team.
  - *How to claim:* None — the credit is applied automatically to the first team on a new account; just sign up at digitalocean.com with a payment method. The current offer is $5/90 days; the previously advertised $200/60-day trial no longer appears anywhere on the official pricing page or in docs.
  - *Expiry:* $5 credit expires 90 days after signup; then normal pay-as-you-go billing begins for anything provisioned.
  - *Gotchas:* Do not expect the old $200/60-day trial — the official pricing/docs pages list only the $5/90-day grant as of today. Payment method required up front, so 'free' is really a trial with a card on file; overage is billed at list price. Stopped Droplets still bill (resources stay reserved) — only destroying them stops charges.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing
- **Always-free platform inclusions (de-facto free tier: static sites, Functions, registry, DNS, firewall, VPC, DOKS control plane)** (high) — Official pricing page 'Platform benefits' (read 2026-10-22): free to build/deploy 3 static sites on App Platform; 90,000 GiB-seconds/month of Functions compute free; free Container Registry with 1 repository and 500 MiB storage; free DNS management; free cloud firewalls; VPC free (ingress free; inter-datacenter VPC peering $0.01/GiB); Droplets include free outbound transfer starting at 500 GiB/month per Droplet; DOKS starts at $12/month with a free control plane. Spaces object storage is NOT free (from $5/month).
  - *Eligibility:* All DigitalOcean accounts; no eligibility filters, no .edu, no card-for-free (a card is required for the account, but the listed free inclusions do not require spending). No published regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* No claiming needed — all existing/new accounts get these inclusions automatically; provision the resources. The 'starting at $0/month' products (App Platform, Functions, Container Registry, VPC) have a free base allowance but paid tiers for usage beyond it.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry — ongoing inclusions as long as the account is active. There is no sunset date published for these allowances; treat them as permanent but subject to change without notice.
  - *Gotchas:* This is not a free-tier substitute for compute: there is no perpetual free Droplet. Free allowances are per-resource (e.g., 500 GiB outbound is per Droplet plan; extra egress beyond bundled allowances is billed — list rate is ~$0.01/GiB per third-party pricing trackers, unverified on the official page today). App Platform's free static sites cap at 3 sites with 1 GiB outbound each.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing
- **AI Startup Ecosystem (Techstars founding partner track) — discounted infrastructure + GPU discounts + infra credits** (medium) — DigitalOcean announced its 'AI Startup Ecosystem' on October 29, 2025 (official blog): it brings together 'established technology partners, vibrant community organizations, AI developer platforms, and university entrepreneurship programs' with Techstars as the named founding partner. Members get 'discounted infrastructure and tooling', technical support and go-to-market resources; the Techstars deal includes 'GPU discounts, infrastructure credits and hands-on technical support', plus hackathon support for early-stage ideas. No dollar amounts are disclosed in the official post — benefits funnel into the Startups program benefits above.
  - *Eligibility:* AI startups (founder-led; early-stage ideas through growth). No .edu requirement; university entrepreneurship programs are partners, not applicant types. No funding cap published for the ecosystem itself (the $10M cap belongs to the core Startups program). No regional limits published.
  - *How to claim:* AI startups apply through the Typeform application linked from the AI Startup Ecosystem announcement (same do.co/AIStartups pipeline as the Startups program). Techstars founders go through Techstars' cohort/support track; partners (VCs, accelerators, university entrepreneurship programs, AI developer platforms) apply separately to join the ecosystem and then route startups to DigitalOcean.
  - *Expiry:* No published end date; amounts/durations are partner-set and evolve. Underlying Startups program credits, if awarded, expire 12 months from acceptance.
  - *Gotchas:* Amounts are not published anywhere official (unlike the $100k press-release figure for Hatch compute credits), so treat any third-party numbers as unverified. 'Discounted infrastructure' is not necessarily free. Application links point to Typeforms that change; verify the current link from the official blog post.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/blog/ai-startup-ecosystem-announcement
- **DigitalOcean Startups program (formerly Hatch) — startup credits** (high) — 12 months of DigitalOcean credits; amount 'varies' per startup/partner and is stated only in the onboarding email, with a general $10,000/month consumption cap (usage beyond the cap in a month is charged at published prices). Credits cover nearly all DO services (Droplets/VPS, App Platform, Kubernetes, Spaces, managed databases, CPU bare metal) but as of 2026-05-13 EXCLUDE: GPU Droplets, NVIDIA H100 GPU Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, Dedicated Inference, Serverless Inference, third-party AI models outside DO infrastructure (incl. Anthropic/OpenAI), Paperspace Machines, Cloudways, and pass-through third-party charges. GPU credits are a separate, by-invitation benefit ('Early-Stage Boost'). Also includes 15 months of free Standard-tier paid support, expert 1:1s with DO PMs/engineers, Marketplace access, and a Welcome Kit partner perks: Stripe Atlas 20% off, Shortcut 2 months free, Cloud66 $500, Ovvy 90 days free, QuickNode up to $5,000, Bolster $1,000, Mercury $500 cash on $50k deposit, Airtable $2,000, Notion $1,000, HubSpot up to 90% off year one, Miro $1,000, Chargebee free until $1M revenue, 100ms $2,500. Migration assistance/credits for moving customers from other clouds (amounts unpublished).
  - *Eligibility:* Must have raised $10M or less total funding; product/digital startups only (service-based businesses: agencies, consulting, web/app dev firms are not eligible); AI-native startups are prioritized but AI is not required; corporate website with a matching company-domain email required (personal email fails); a registered DigitalOcean team account (company email, not personal) required; applicants must be new to DO credits (no prior DO credit use); a valid credit card must be on file; no geographic restrictions published (global). No .edu or enrollment requirement.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a DO team account with a company email and valid credit card. 2) Apply at digitalocean.com/startups (about 10-minute application; if unaffiliated with a partner organization, select 'Other'), or via an approved partner (VCs, accelerators, incubators — Techstars is a 2026 premier partner). 3) Acceptance at DO's sole discretion; your credit amount arrives in the onboarding email; benefits are distributed through approved partners.
  - *Expiry:* Credits end exactly 12 months from acceptance; unused credits are forfeited and are not transferable; no extensions, no renewal mechanism (program term fixed at 12 months per ToS, last updated 2026-05-11). DO may change or discontinue the program at any time. Termination events (crypto mining, IPO, insolvency, prohibited change of control) end all benefits immediately and any fees owed become due.
  - *Gotchas:* Headline credit amount is not published ('varies') — the Jan 2025 Hatch press release claimed up to $100,000 in compute credits, but the current page no longer advertises any total; budget against the $10,000/month cap, not a headline number. GPU/inference/third-party AI models are excluded as of 2026-05-13 — a major gotcha for AI workloads. Overage beyond the monthly cap and beyond Droplet bandwidth allowances is billed. Powered-off Droplets still bill. Egress beyond the included bandwidth allowance costs extra. No free trial exists for bare-metal or dedicated servers; they are covered only if your credits apply and usage stays under the monthly cap (bare-metal CPUs eligible; bare-metal GPUs excluded).
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/startups/
- **New-account signup credit ($5 / 90 days)** (high) — New team accounts automatically receive a $5 credit applied to the first team; valid for 90 days after signup. The pricing page states your card will not be charged until the credit is exhausted or the 90-day window lapses (e.g., spending $3 in the window means no charge).
  - *Eligibility:* All new DigitalOcean accounts; no .edu, student or startup requirement; no region limit published; a payment method (card) IS required before creating resources — so this is a trial credit, not a no-card free tier.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up for a DigitalOcean account, add a payment card; the $5 credit is applied automatically to the first team. No application needed.
  - *Expiry:* 90 days from signup.
  - *Gotchas:* Card required (not free-tier in the no-CC sense). $5 covers roughly 1-2 months of the cheapest $4/mo Droplet; anything beyond it bills immediately. Historically larger trials (e.g. $100-$200/60-day offers) are not shown on the current pricing page as of 2026-10-22.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing
- **Always-free tier items (no perpetual free VPS)** (high) — Perpetually free with any account: 3 free static sites on App Platform (~1 GiB outbound/mo each); 90,000 GiB-seconds/mo free on DO Functions with no charge for invocations; Container Registry free with 1 repository and 500 MiB storage; unlimited VPCs at no cost with free ingress data transfer to VPCs; Droplets include free outbound data transfer starting at 500 GiB/mo; free reserved IPs when assigned to a Droplet; free DNS; free Cloud Firewalls; free basic monitoring/alerts; 1 uptime check/mo; free email support tier. Managed databases and basic Droplets are NOT free (start at $15/mo and $4/mo respectively). No free-trial offer for bare metal or dedicated servers appears anywhere on the pricing page.
  - *Eligibility:* All DigitalOcean account holders; no .edu, no startup requirement; payment method still required for any billable resource.
  - *How to claim:* Included automatically with any account; no application or claim step.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry (ongoing free allowances).
  - *Gotchas:* Droplets themselves are never free — so there is no free VPS; 'free outbound transfer starting at 500 GiB/mo' scales with the Droplet plan. Static-site free tier egress is ~1 GiB/mo per site; larger traffic bills. VPC peering between datacenters is $0.01/GiB (intra-DC free). Powered-off Droplets continue to bill.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing
- **Ad-hoc university partnership grants (case-by-case, not a general education license)** (medium) — DigitalOcean makes individual university grants: e.g. the University of Arizona Eller MIS Department (announced July 2025) partners with DO to provide unlimited cloud resources to students in one master's-level cloud computing class for the duration of the semester. This is a bespoke grant, not a claimable .edu program.
  - *Eligibility:* Only students in specific partner classes/programs, at the discretion of the DO-teacher partnership; no general .edu email or student verification pathway exists as of 2026-10-22 (the old GitHub Student Pack credit is gone — see sunset notes).
  - *How to claim:* No public application; arises only when a professor/department negotiates with DigitalOcean.
  - *Expiry:* Per-partnership (e.g., one semester in the Eller case).
  - *Gotchas:* Not renewable, not generally available, not tied to .edu emails; the former blanket student offer ($200 via GitHub Student Developer Pack) was discontinued.
  - *Source:* https://eller.arizona.edu/news/eller-mis-department-partners-digitalocean-serve-students
- **Automatic $5 / 90-day signup credit (new accounts)** (high) — Per official docs: 'When you create your first team, DigitalOcean automatically applies a $5 signup credit.' No promo code required. The credit applies to all products except SaaS Add-Ons — so it covers Droplets (VPS), bare metal and dedicated servers, App Platform, etc. A valid payment method must be on file before you can create resources; your card is charged only after the credit is exhausted or the 90-day window expires ('if you received a $5 credit for 90 days, then that $5 credit is automatically applied to your account' — pricing FAQ). Credit expires 90 days after signup; it cannot be transferred to other teams. Governing 'Promotional Credit & Discount Terms' (last updated Jun 7 2024): no cash value, non-transferable, no stacking with other promotions, exclusions for Marketplace/Support/crypto mining, voiding for accounts created solely to obtain credits. Docs page last verified 15 Jul 2026.
  - *Eligibility:* All new accounts — any person/company's first DigitalOcean team. No student or startup status needed, no .edu email. A credit card or other valid payment method (Visa, Mastercard, Amex, Discover, PayPal, Google/Apple Pay) is required on file, so it is NOT a no-credit-card offer; billing only happens after credit exhaustion or the 90-day expiry. No region limits stated on the page.
  - *How to claim:* Automatic: create your first team; the $5 credit is applied with no code and no application. Add a payment method first. Claim: free on everything non-SaaS Add-On up to $5.
  - *Expiry:* 90 days from account signup; no renewal; one-time only (first team only, non-transferable to later teams).
  - *Gotchas:* Payment method required (trial-style, not card-free). Amount is small ($5) vs the wound-down former $200/60-day trial. 'All products except SaaS Add-Ons' still covers bare metal and dedicated, but DO Marketplace products and DO Support are excluded from promotional credits per the legal terms. Overage beyond $5 (or any spend after day 90) bills normally; free outbound bandwidth allowance (e.g., 500 GiB/mo on small Droplets) and free reserved IP/DNS are separate product inclusions, not credit. Fraud/accounts created solely for credits are voided.
  - *Source:* https://docs.digitalocean.com/platform/billing/signup-credit/(corroboratedbyhttps://www.digitalocean.com/pricing/FAQandhttps://www.digitalocean.com/legal/promotional-credit-discount-terms)
- **DigitalOcean Startup Program (startup credits via partner networks)** (high) — Official program page (fetched today): rolling 12-month platform-credit window; credit amount 'varies' and is determined through approved partner organizations/accelerators; participants 'are entitled to a $10,000 monthly limit' beyond which standard charges apply. GPU credits exist separately for select applicants after review. Enrolled startups also get free access to Standard-tier paid support for 15 months and discounted third-party perks (e.g., Stripe Atlas 20% off, QuickNode up to $5k, Airtable $2,000, Miro $1,000). As of May 13 2026, credits explicitly EXCLUDE: GPU Droplets, NVIDIA H100 GPU Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, Dedicated Inference, Serverless Inference, third-party AI models (Anthropic, OpenAI), Paperspace Machines, Cloudways, and pass-through third-party charges; crypto mining and excessive resource consumption prohibited; credits non-transferable. Co-branded with Y Combinator, Techstars, a16z, Backstage Capital, etc. 'The DigitalOcean Startups program does not grant extensions' and unused credits are 'forfeited'.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups only: must have raised $10m or less; a company website with a matching corporate email; registered DO team account on a business (not personal) email; program targets 'new startups that have not used prior credits with DigitalOcean' and 'prioritizes AI-native startups'; service-based companies (consulting, web/app dev services, marketing agencies) are NOT eligible. No company-age floor, employee cap, or geographic restriction stated. No .edu requirement.
  - *How to claim:* Two paths: (1) if backed by a partner/accelerator on the portfolio list, route through that partner's program manager; (2) otherwise fill out the application via the Typeform on the /startups page and select 'Other' as the affiliated partner. Once accepted, perks are delivered via the 'Welcome Kit'. The partner determines the actual credit amount (undisclosed publicly; $10k/month cap on usage).
  - *Expiry:* Rolling 12-month credit window from enrollment; no extensions granted; unused credits forfeited at window end; no calendar application deadline published (rolling eligibility).
  - *Gotchas:* Exact credit amount is undisclosed ('varies' by partner) — treat any figure circulating ($10k–$250k historically) as unverified. GPU and bare-metal-GPU products and third-party AI model spend are excluded from credits (as of May 13 2026), so this program does NOT reliably fund GPU/dedicated AI hardware. Non-transferable, no stacking with the signup credit is addressed in the Promo Credit terms. 15-month support perk is Standard tier only.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/startups/
- **Wavemakers Program (community chapter leaders — open, not student/startup-specific)** (medium) — DigitalOcean-supported local community chapters; leaders 'Get access to DO credits to use on our platform' (no dollar amount or duration published), plus exclusive swag, invite-only events/workshops, and web/social features. Leaders must host at least four events per year (one per quarter, virtual or in-person), serve as the main point of contact, use the designated event platform, and stay in touch with DO. FAQ: open to 'tech enthusiasts, community builders, and influencers from all backgrounds' and worldwide (U.S. not required).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone who wants to lead a local DO community chapter — not restricted to students or startups. No .edu or company requirement; global.
  - *How to claim:* As member: join via the linked Meetup. As chapter leader: (1) fill out the quick application (Google Form), (2) DO team reviews and contacts you with next steps, (3) receive a welcome packet including a community leader toolkit; credits arrive through DO's side once established.
  - *Expiry:* No application deadline, credit expiry, or duration published on the page; ongoing rolling program.
  - *Gotchas:* Credit amount, duration, and product scope are NOT disclosed — low value certainty; it's primarily a community commitment (4 events/year). Do not treat this as a budget-grade credit source.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/wavemakers/
- **Always-free product tiers and add-ons (all accounts, incl. VPS customers)** (high) — From the official pricing page (fetched today): 'Build and deploy 3 static sites for free' (App Platform static sites); 'One Uptime Check/month included with every account'; Kubernetes: 'Free control plane, free bandwidth allowance' (node plans from $12/mo); free VPCs (unlimited, intra-DC peering free); free reserved IPs when attached to a Droplet, free DNS, free cloud firewalls; Container Registry: 'Free container registry with 1 repository and 500 MiB storage'; Functions free tier as printed on the pricing page: 'Use 90,000 GiB-seconds per month for free with DigitalOcean Functions' and 'no additional charges for function invocations'; Droplets include free outbound transfer starting at 500 GiB/mo (overage $0.01/GiB). NOTE: there is no free Droplet/VPS tier — the $5/90-day signup credit is the only standing free-usage grant for VPS.
  - *Eligibility:* All accounts, no application, no student/startup status. No .edu requirement; no region limit stated. No credit card required to hold the free add-ons (a free-tier-only usage of, e.g., a static site or registry can be exercised without spend).
  - *How to claim:* Inherent: provision the resources in the control panel (static site on App Platform, registry, VPC, K8s free control plane); nothing to apply for.
  - *Expiry:* Perpetual; no published expiry or renewal cycle.
  - *Gotchas:* The Functions '90,000 GiB-seconds' figure is exactly as printed on /pricing/ today but could not be cross-checked against a /functions/ product page (404 at fetch time) — medium certainty on that number. Free static sites: 3 sites cap; app (dynamic) sites on App Platform are not free. Free K8s control plane still requires paid nodes (from $12/mo). The often-cited '$5/mo for 250 GB Spaces' is a paid entry price, not a free offer. Free egress allowances attach to specific Droplet sizes; bare metal/dedicated have their own plans (no free tier).
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing/
- **Education / student or educator programs — none currently published (negative finding)** (medium) — No education license, student program, or educator discount is published on DigitalOcean's site as of today: /education/, /educators/, /community/education/, docs /platform/education/, and blog tag /education/ all return HTTP 404 (verified by fetch today). The pricing page FAQ contains no student/education offers, and the legal index shows no education-specific credit terms. The only standing free grant for new users (any user) is the $5/90-day signup credit.
  - *Eligibility:* N/A — no current education offer to be eligible for. .edu email would not unlock anything specifically.
  - *How to claim:* N/A — nothing to claim; students effectively get the same $5/90-day signup credit as any new account (and Startup/Wavemakers programs if they meet those programs' criteria).
  - *Expiry:* N/A.
  - *Gotchas:* Absence verified by direct URL checks today, but WebSearch was unavailable in this session (budget exhausted), so I could not confirm via third-party news whether an education program existed in 2025 and was quietly removed.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing/(noeducationmention)+404checkson/education/,/educators/,/community/education/,/blog/tag/education/
- **New-account free trial: $200 credit for 60 days** (medium) — $200 in cloud credit valid for 60 days for new DigitalOcean accounts. Auto-applied at signup; usable across Droplets, App Platform, managed databases, Kubernetes, and Spaces. Marketplace/third-party integrations are excluded. Credits are consumed before the card is charged. After 60 days or when the credit is exhausted, standard pay-as-you-go billing applies and any unused credit is forfeited.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to new accounts in general - NOT restricted to .edu or students. Requires a valid credit card or PayPal for identity verification (no charge during the trial unless the credit is exhausted). One trial per person. No funding/age cap; not startup-specific.
  - *How to claim:* Create a new DigitalOcean account (email, Google, or GitHub), add a credit card/PayPal, and the $200 credit is automatically applied to billing. Deploy workloads (Droplet/App Platform/DB/K8s). A referral or promo link can be used for the trial. Set a reminder before day 60.
  - *Expiry:* 60 days from account creation; unused credit forfeited at expiry; no renewal or extension.
  - *Gotchas:* A payment card IS required, so it is a card-required trial, not a no-card free tier. Trial-vs-free: every running resource becomes billable after 60 days. Egress beyond included allowances and all marketplace/third-party services are billed. Region availability of specific services varies. NOTE: the official /free-trial/ landing page returns a 404 today; the $200/60-day terms are corroborated by current (2026) third-party and first-party community sources, not a live official trial page.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/community/questions/signup-and-get-200-in-credit-for-your-first-60-days-cffec92b-5b4a-44ba-88df-4e0c8ccee7ea
- **GitHub Student Developer Pack - $200 student credit (SUNSET / no longer listed)** (medium) — Historically, students verified through GitHub (Student Developer Pack, typically .edu) could claim $200 of DigitalOcean credit. As of today's check, DigitalOcean is NOT listed as a perk in the current GitHub Student Developer Pack, and the dedicated DigitalOcean student page (/github-students) now redirects to the generic homepage. Community reports (May 2026) indicate the credit stopped applying after GitHub authorization + card. No active standalone .edu-only student-credit program could be confirmed. Practical effect: the only currently-usable student path is the general $200/60-day new-account trial above.
  - *Eligibility:* Previously .edu / verified GitHub student status; now appears unavailable. No live activation path found.
  - *How to claim:* Previously: verify GitHub student status, then activate DigitalOcean from the Student Developer Pack. Now: no working activation path found - confirm on the live GitHub pack page before expecting any credit.
  - *Expiry:* Appears discontinued; no stated end date; the pack no longer lists DigitalOcean.
  - *Gotchas:* Do not rely on this offer. The 'known so far' $200 GitHub-pack student credit should be treated as outdated. If re-activated later, it would again likely require a card for account verification.
  - *Source:* https://education.github.com/pack
- **DigitalOcean Startups program (credits for early-stage startups)** (high) — Participants receive a pre-determined credit amount (varies; spelled out in the onboarding email) subject to a $10,000 monthly spend ceiling, valid over a 12-month term (i.e., up to ~$120K of core credits). Credits apply to qualifying DigitalOcean services but EXCLUDE GPU Droplets, NVIDIA H100 GPU Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, Dedicated/Serverless Inference, third-party/frontier AI models (including Anthropic and OpenAI), Paperspace Machines, Cloudways, and third-party pass-through charges. GPU credits are a separate, non-guaranteed invitation after review. Standard paid support is free for 15 months.
  - *Eligibility:* For new startups that have NOT used prior DigitalOcean credits. Must have raised $10M or less. AI-native startups prioritized; service firms (agencies/consulting) excluded. Requires a corporate/business email, a registered DigitalOcean team account, a valid credit card, and a public company website. No stated company-age or geographic restriction.
  - *How to claim:* VC/accelerator-backed: confirm the partnership via DigitalOcean's portfolio partner list and ask your program manager. Independent founders: submit the application (Typeform) and select 'Other' for the affiliated partner organization. Perks are delivered in the 'Welcome Kit' on acceptance.
  - *Expiry:* 12-month program term; no extensions granted; unused credits forfeited; credits are non-transferable.
  - *Gotchas:* A credit card is required to be eligible. The widely-circulated 'up to $250K' figure is NOT what the current official page states - the current framing is a $10,000/month ceiling over 12 months with 'amount varies' (historically marketed as up to ~$200K including partner credits). Effective May 13, 2026 the eligible-service list broadened (core credits apply to more services, still excluding the GPU/AI-model/third-party list above). Prohibited uses include cryptocurrency mining and workloads with excessive network/CPU/disk I/O.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/startups/
- **Always-free services (free tier): static sites on App Platform + included add-ons** (high) — Up to 3 static sites can be built and deployed on App Platform for free with no expiration (~1 GiB outbound transfer per app; additional static apps cost $3/month). App Platform entry is listed as '$0/month'. Also always-free for all accounts: DNS management, Cloud Firewalls, monitoring & alerting, one free Container Registry repository (500 MiB), and free round-the-clock technical support.
  - *Eligibility:* All DigitalOcean accounts - not student/startup-specific. No .edu or funding requirements.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up (with the $200 trial or a standard account); deploy static sites for free; the free add-ons are included automatically with a paid account.
  - *Expiry:* No expiration on the free static-site allowance; free add-ons are ongoing with a paid account.
  - *Gotchas:* Only STATIC apps are free - dynamic App Platform apps bill by resource units. Free static outbound transfer is capped (~1 GiB/app/month); egress beyond included allowances and third-party/marketplace services are billed. This free tier is separate from, and smaller than, the startup credits.
  - *Source:* https://www.digitalocean.com/pricing/
- **2025–26 change:** 1) No student/education offer found: as of 2026-10-22 DigitalOcean has no public student, .edu, or education-license program. The URL requested in the task (digitalocean.com/community/solutions/digitalocean-for-startups) and a guessed .../digitalocean-for-students both return HTTP 404; the startup program now lives at digitalocean.com/startups. No .edu email requirement appears anywhere in official pages. 2) Program rebrand + duration change: 'Hatch' was rebranded 'DigitalOcean Startups' with a new 2025 benefits package announced Jan 22, 2025 (investors.digitalocean.com press release + BusinessWire). The previously referenced 'up to $100,000 over 24 months' is superseded: the official ToS (last updated May 11, 2026) sets a 12-month program term and a 1-year credit window, and the current page states amounts 'vary' per partner with a $10,000 monthly limit. 3) May 13, 2026 restriction expansion: core startup credits now explicitly exclude GPU Droplets, NVIDIA H100 GPU Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, Dedicated Inference, Serverless Inference/third-party AI models, Paperspace Machines and Cloudways (previously only GPU Droplets and H100 products were excluded); GPU support moved fully to the invite-only 'Early-Stage Boost' packages, and the Jan 2025 'up to 3 months free GPU usage' language is no longer published. 4) Support wording changed: 'Complimentary Premium Support' (Jan 2025 release) is now '15 months of free Standard-tier support'. 5) The older $200/60-day new-account trial no longer appears on the official pricing page or docs (docs.digitalocean.com/resources/free-trial/ 404s); the current trial is a $5/90-day credit automatically applied to a new account. 6) Oct 29, 2025: AI Startup Ecosystem launched with Techstars as founding partner — no published amounts, funnels into the Startups program.
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025-2026 changes: (1) HATCH RETIRED — the Hatch brand was retired and replaced by the "DigitalOcean Startups" program; the Hatch ToS now only point to a prior version of the Startups ToS (Startups ToS last updated May 11, 2026; third-party guides place the brand retirement on May 11, 2026). The Jan 2025 Hatch offer of "up to $100,000 in compute credits + up to 3 months free GPU usage" (announced Jan 22, 2025) is no longer the current offer — the current page says credit amount 'varies' with a $10,000/month cap and unpublished totals. (2) STUDENT CREDIT ENDED — DigitalOcean exited the GitHub Student Developer Pack; students were told around July 1, 2026 that DO was "winding down the program" and "ending their participation in the GitHub Student Developer Pack"; ALL Student Pack promotional credits, including ones already redeemed that had run a full year, expired universally on July 31, 2026 (retroactive cutoff). From June 8, 2026 the credit had already excluded GPU Droplets, H100 Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, DO Inference, third-party AI models, Paperspace and Cloudways. There is no replacement DigitalOcean student/education license as of 2026-10-22. (3) CREDIT COVERAGE NARROWED — as of May 13, 2026 the Startups program credits explicitly exclude Dedicated/Serverless Inference, third-party AI models, Paperspace Machines, Cloudways and Bare Metal GPUs (in addition to the earlier GPU Droplet/H100 exclusions), and the page now states the $10,000/month cap. (4) A limit reduction was recorded around 2026-04-07 (narrowed signup-credit terms; low confidence, from a third-party changelog). (5) No free bare-metal or dedicated-server trial, no perpetual free VPS tier, and no no-credit-card offer exists as of 2026-10-22 — a payment card is required for every DO offer above.
- **2025–26 change:** Sunset/change notes for 2025–2026: (1) The former $200/60-day new-user trial is wound down — the old trial landing pages (/free-trial/ and /trial/) now 404 (verified today), and it has been replaced by the small automatic $5/90-day signup credit applied to the first team (docs page last verified 15 Jul 2026). (2) Startup Program: effective May 13 2026, credits were re-scoped to EXCLUDE GPU Droplets, NVIDIA H100 GPU Kubernetes, Bare Metal GPUs, Dedicated Inference, Serverless Inference, third-party AI models (Anthropic/OpenAI), Paperspace and Cloudways — so startup credits no longer fund GPU/bare-metal AI hardware; program also explicitly grants no extensions and forfeits unused credits. (3) Wavemakers is DigitalOcean's current community-chapter program (replacing/continuing the older 'DigitalOcean Community Chapter' branding) with unpublished credit amounts. (4) No evidence of any education/student program in the 2025–2026 official site; the Promotional Credit & Discount Terms page is unchanged since June 7 2024. Method caveat: WebSearch was unavailable for this session (shared budget exhausted), so the '2025-2026 changes' cross-check relied solely on WebFetch of DigitalOcean's official pages; third-party press coverage was not verified.
- **2025–26 change:** GitHub Student Developer Pack $200 student credit: DigitalOcean is no longer listed as a perk in the current GitHub Student Developer Pack (checked 2026-10-22 via education.github.com/pack), and the dedicated DigitalOcean /github-students page now redirects to the generic homepage. Community reports (May 2026) say the credit stopped applying after GitHub authorization + adding a card. Treat the 'known so far' $200 GitHub-pack student credit as outdated/unavailable; the only live student-accessible path today is the general $200/60-day new-account trial.DO Startups credit framing changed materially: earlier 'known so far' cited 'up to $250K in credits.' The current official page (checked 2026-10-22) does NOT print a headline total; it frames the benefit as a pre-determined amount ('varies', via onboarding email) with a $10,000 monthly ceiling over 12 months (max ~$120K of core credits), and the eligible-service list was broadened effective May 13, 2026. The $250K figure is unsupported by the current official page.The official /free-trial/ landing page returns a 404 today, so the $200/60-day trial could not be read on a live official page; it remains corroborated by current (2026) third-party guides and first-party DigitalOcean community threads. No active .edu-only education license program was found.
- **Critic correction** (Standing new-account credit is ~$5 for 90 days ...; the former $200/60-day ...): DigitalOcean's new-account promotion is $200 in credit for the first 60 days, new accounts only, per its promo text on community pages. The $5 for 90 days is a separate AI-inference trial. My homepage fetch showed no banner, so this rests on the community-page text and third-party listings.
- **Critic correction** (conflicting: '$200 / 60-day' claim versus official 90-day signup-credit expiry): The official signup-credit doc (last verified 2026-07-15) says credits expire 90 days after signup and a payment method is needed before creating Droplets. The Free Trial entry says about $5 for 90 days, while the student/startup entry still says $200 for 60 days. The official excerpt read did not state an amount, so confirm it there. A secondary source (October 2026) says the GitHub Student Pack credit ended 2026-07-31; that is unverified and the $200 student claim needs re-checking.

### Google Cloud

- **Google Cloud Free Trial — $300 welcome credit (90 days)** (high) — New customers get $300 free credit, valid 90 days from Free Trial billing account creation. Credit covers any product including overages beyond Free Tier limits; it does not roll over (expires 90 days from signup even if you upgrade to paid). Free Trial billing account auto-closes when the $300 is spent or 90 days pass unless you manually upgrade to a Paid billing account (then you keep remaining credit until the 90-day mark and keep Free Tier access). Eligibility: you must have NEVER been a paying user of Google Cloud, Google Maps Platform, or Firebase, and never signed up for the Free Trial before (one per person/billing profile). GPUs, Windows servers and some features are not covered. During trial you are not billed — only if you manually upgrade to paid do charges begin. 30-day data-export window after the trial ends. Compute Engine trial restricted to 8 concurrent cores.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone, any country with GCP access (no .edu or startup status needed). Credit card/other payment method REQUIRED at signup for identity verification (Google may run a pending authorization, not a charge). US/Mexico government entities excluded.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at cloud.google.com (Get started for free) -> add card -> $300 credit is preloaded into the Free Trial billing account automatically; spend over 90 days on any product incl. Vertex AI/Gemini API; optionally click Upgrade in console to keep credit alive beyond trial features. Do NOT click 'Activate a paid account' unless you want charges.
  - *Expiry:* 90 days from signup or until $300 is exhausted, whichever first; unused credit does not survive account closure. Free Tier usage continues if you convert to a paid account.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial vs Free Tier: the $300 is for trial billing only; Free Tier allowances are separate, not charged against it. CC required but you are only charged if you manually upgrade to paid. Egress, third-party models (e.g. non-Google LLMs on Vertex) cost above free allowances. 30-day migration window after end; GCE resources may be deleted and are not recoverable.
  - *Source:* https://docs.cloud.google.com/free/docs/free-cloud-features
- **Google Cloud Free Tier — 20+ always-free products (no expiry)** (high) — Per-product monthly free allowances, reset each month, do NOT expire but limits are 'subject to change'. Verified examples from cloud.google.com/free: Compute Engine 1 e2-micro/month; Cloud Storage 5 GB Standard; BigQuery 1 TB scanned/month; Cloud Run 2M requests + Cloud Run Functions 2M invocations/month; GKE 1 Autopilot/zonal cluster; Pub/Sub 10 GB messages; Vision AI 1,000 units; Speech-to-Text 60 min; Natural Language 5,000 units; Cloud Build 120 build-minutes/day; KMS Autokey 100 active key versions + 10k ops; Artifact Registry, Firestore 1 GB, Secret Manager 6 versions, Cloud Shell 5 GB. Includes 'AI APIs' free usage — the GCP-hosted AI endpoints (Vertex AI GenAI preview limits apply separately).
