No program duration, renewal, or product exclusions are published on the page. Read directly from the official page today.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups with recent Series A–E funding (institutional VC backing implied); registered company; no .edu or small-startup/seed stage allowed — post-Series A only
  - *How to claim:* Submit the application on the Vultr Discover startup program page (Apply Now form / Join Now link) with company details, CTO and cloud-infra-lead contacts, 6 months of cloud-compute invoices, Series A–E proof, and public-reference consent; Vultr evaluates and activates credits on acceptance.
  - *Expiry:* No credit validity period published; 'long-term' discounts imply a commitment; subject to Vultr internal approval
  - *Gotchas:* 'Up to' amounts — actual credit set case by case. Requires public referenceability and sharing 6 months of invoices (operational transparency). Not self-service: evaluation by a Vultr team. No stated end date, but approval is discretionary.
  - *Source:* https://discover.vultr.com/startup-program
- **Partner-hub startup credits: $2,500 (6 months), advanced up to $45K for Series C+** (medium) — Partner ecosystem offers (India startup hubs): $2,500 in Vultr cloud credits, valid 6 months, applied at full list price; quarterly expert training sessions; group onboarding; up to 20% post-credit service discounts subject to a 12-month service agreement; CPU services only (GPU excluded per GL Bajaj terms). Eligibility determined jointly by Vultr and the hub; approval final. Advanced Credit Program: Series C and above may get up to $45,000 subject to evaluation/internal approval.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups associated with the specific partner hub (IIT Mandi, MeitY Startup Hub, KNIT Sultanpur, GL Bajaj, T-Hub, DPIIT); no .edu requirement; no explicit company age cap; Series C+ for the $45K advanced tier
  - *How to claim:* Apply through the partner hub (IIT Mandi / MeitY MSH / KNIT Sultanpur / GL Bajaj / T-Hub — via discover.vultr.com partner pages); hub and Vultr jointly determine eligibility; Vultr activates the $2,500 credit; Series C+ startups can request evaluation for up to $45,000 under the advanced program.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid 6 months; discounts available only after credit period and require a 12-month agreement; no renewal stated
  - *Gotchas:* India-partner scoped — non-affiliated companies should use the main Digital Start-up Program instead. Credits are CPU-only, not GPU. Joint approval with the hub (not pure self-service). After 6 months you need a 12-month commitment to keep the 20% discount.
  - *Source:* https://discover.vultr.com/meity-startup-hub-msh
- **Open Source Credits program (Sep 2026, amount undisclosed)** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — New listing dated Sep 30, 2026 on Vultr's Offers & Programs hub: 'Credits for the services behind your project: CI runners, artifact storage, package distribution, and production workloads, available via API, CLI, and Terraform.' The overview page publishes no amount, eligibility rules, application steps, or expiration. There is also a 'Free Vultr Credits for MLH Hackathons' listing (Aug 19, 2026) with no amount published.
  - *Eligibility:* Not published on the hub page — presumed open-source project maintainers; confirm on the sub-page
  - *How to claim:* Details live on the sub-page (discover.vultr.com/open-source-credits); sign up/apply there for an open-source project; credits delivered via Vultr API/CLI/Terraform integrations.
  - *Expiry:* Not published
  - *Gotchas:* Cannot be confirmed from official listing alone — amount/limits unknown; treat as lead-gen page. Same for MLH hackathon credits (student-adjacent, no published amount).
  - *Source:* https://discover.vultr.com/offers
- **2025–26 change:** 1) No official dollar figure: Vultr's docs (updated Nov 18, 2025) say only 'free credits for up to 30 days'; the $100 base is a promo-link convention, and 2025-2026 official coupon listings swung to $200/$250/$300 — the exact amount always tracks the specific signup link/coupon, not a standing program. 2) The .edu $5 student credit appears to be a legacy/sunset offer: it is cited by third-party coupon sites (2019-2023) but absent from all current official Vultr promo/coupons/docs pages; treat as unverified for 2026. 3) VULTRMATCH and 50off both carry explicit 'limited time' disclaimers — these rotating coupons can end without notice; VULTRMATCH credit expires 12 months after issuance. 4) The Free Tier Program (1vCPU/512MB/10GB in Miami/Seattle/Frankfurt) is a phased, randomized-access rollout with 'no hard dates' — it can be adjusted or closed at Vultr's discretion. 5) No student-specific program, education license, or university discount page exists on official Vultr property in 2025-2026 (historically a 2014 'College Students can try us Free!' blog post and ad-hoc academic class partnerships, e.g. Stony Brook CSE 356). 6) No material 2025-2026 sunset of the trial itself was found; the main 2025-2026 changes are coupon rotation ($100 -> $200/$250/$300), the new Open Source Credits listing (Sep 30, 2026), and expansion of India partner-hub startup programs.
